## 任务：在 bjb1 上启动第三个 1.7B episode-level GRPO seed 训练

### 背景
需要第三个 seed 的 OOD 数据（Seed 1: 23.88%, Seed 3: 10.45%，差异大）。在 bjb1 cuda:0,1 上训练，超参与 Seed 1/3 完全相同。

### 第一步：检查 bjb1 上的基础设施（ssh_read_only on bjb1-17606）

```bash
# SFT checkpoint 是否已在 bjb1 上
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen3_exp021_ragen_sft_v4/ 2>/dev/null | head -5

# RAGEN 代码是否已部署
ls /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/train.py 2>/dev/null && echo "RAGEN_OK" || echo "NO_RAGEN"

# 现有配置参考
ls /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/verl/examples/config/_alfworld_exp029*.yaml 2>/dev/null
# 或新路径
ls /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/config/_alfworld_exp029*.yaml 2>/dev/null

# ALFWorld 数据
ls /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/data/ 2>/dev/null | head -10

# 磁盘空间
df -h /root/autodl-tmp
```

### 第二步：如果 SFT checkpoint 不在 bjb1 上

从 westd scp 过来。先检查 westd→bjb1 或 bjb1→westd 的 SSH 连通性：
```bash
# 在 bjb1 上试连 westd
ssh -o ConnectTimeout=5 westd-47453 "echo OK" 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_DIRECT_SSH"
```

如果不能直连，需要通过本地中转。SFT checkpoint 路径在 westd: `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen3_exp021_ragen_sft_v4/`

### 第三步：读取 Seed 3 训练配置作为参考（ssh_read_only on westd-47453）

```bash
cat /root/autodl-tmp/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/config/_alfworld_exp029_seed3_ood_final.yaml
# 或原始训练配置
cat /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/verl/examples/config/_alfworld_exp029_episode_level.yaml 2>/dev/null
cat /root/autodl-tmp/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/config/_alfworld_exp029_episode_level.yaml 2>/dev/null
```

### 第四步：在 bjb1 上创建 Seed 4 训练配置（ssh_execute on bjb1-17606）

创建配置文件（路径按 bjb1 上 RAGEN 的实际 config 目录）。

关键参数（必须与 Seed 1/3 一致）：
- lr: 1e-6
- kl_coef (β): 0.01
- gamma (γ): 0.95
- group_size (K): 8
- total_training_steps: 15
- context_window_mode: full（episode-level）
- n_gpus_per_node: 2
- **optimizer_offload: false**（关键！避免 eval 时 device mismatch）
- save_freq: 5（保存 step 5, 10, 15）

与 Seed 1/3 不同的部分：
- seed.train: 123, seed.val: 456（或其他未使用过的值。已用: seed1 用默认, seed3 用 42/123）
- experiment_name: exp029-seed4-episode-level
- checkpoint 输出路径: /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/exp029-seed4/
- log 路径相应修改

### 第五步：提交训练（submit_training_job）

- exp_id: exp029_seed4_episode
- server: bjb1-17606
- gpu_ids: ["cuda:0", "cuda:1"]
- 命令:
```
bash -c "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && export TMPDIR=/root/autodl-tmp/tmp && mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/tmp && cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1 WANDB_PROJECT=cost-verify-agent-rl python train.py --config-name _alfworld_exp029_seed4_episode"
```
- timeout: 28800 (8h)

### 第六步：验证启动（ssh_read_only）
```bash
sleep 30 && tail -20 ~/runs/cost-verify-agent-rl/exp029_seed4_episode.log 2>/dev/null | grep -v "WARNING.*Skipping"
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used --format=csv,noheader
```

### 第七步：更新 registry
- exp_id: exp029_seed4_episode
- hypothesis: "Episode-level GRPO avoids collapse and learns consistently across seeds (seed 4, seed=123). Third seed replication for robustness evidence. Seed 1 OOD=23.88%, Seed 3 OOD=10.45%."
- parent: exp029_seed3_v2（同系列）
- status: RUNNING with full spec

### 注意
- optimizer_offload 必须设为 false
- 如果 bjb1 上缺 SFT checkpoint 或 ALFWorld 数据，需要先传输
- seed 值选 123/456（确认之前没用过这个组合）

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
